Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a trees algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 389. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 117/3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the trees space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 389 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 117. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in trees theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 554 and the invariant I(A,B) = 439 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 554 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the trees structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 439. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
